# Advancements

Codecs, pure and live matching, progression, ordered reward delivery, game rules, packets and persistence adapters are implemented. TICK and the native 20-tick LOCATION tail are wired; item, sculk and crafter producer integration remains in progress. Identity-set child order is captured for exact layout comparison; runtime identity collections use deterministic insertion order.

Mechanics are identified from the 26.3 server source. Registry and resource entries are mechanically enumerated from the official reports, bootstrapped registries and built-in datapack.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| advancements.criteria.progress-dates | Grant and revoke criteria with timestamps and persist progress across restart | `PlayerAdvancements#award; PlayerAdvancements#revoke` | `advancements::tests::vanilla_progress_award_revoke_dates_and_wire` | pass | partial |
| advancements.rewards.once | Grant configured XP, recipes, loot and reward function when completion changes | `AdvancementRewards#grant; PlayerAdvancements#award` | `advancements::tests::vanilla_player_listeners_visibility_packets_rewards_and_persistence` | pass | partial |
